{
  OBJECT-PROPERTIES
  {

    Liste versions=CMC02.01.00;

  PROPERTIES
  {
    CaptionML=[ENU=Purchase - Credit Memo;
               FRA=Achats : Avoir fournisseur];
    TopMargin=500;
    BottomMargin=200;
    LeftMargin=500;
    OnInitReport=BEGIN
                   GLSetup.GET;
                 END;

    OnPreReport=BEGIN
                  IF NOT CurrReport.USEREQUESTFORM THEN
                    InitLogInteraction;
                END;

    Orientation=Landscape;
    HorzGrid=210;
  }
  DATAITEMS
  {
    { PROPERTIES
      {
        DataItemTable=Table124;
        DataItemTableView=SORTING(No.)
                          WHERE(Collection Invoice Type=FILTER(Collection|Supplement));
        NewPagePerRecord=Oui;
        ReqFilterHeadingML=[ENU=Posted Purchase Cr. Memo;
                            FRA=Avoir achat enregistr'];
        OnAfterGetRecord=VAR
                           L_i@8053000 : Integer;
                         BEGIN
                           CurrReport.LANGUAGE := Language.GetLanguageID("Language Code");

                           CompanyInfo.GET;

                           IF RespCenter.GET("Responsibility Center") THEN BEGIN
                             FormatAddr.RespCenter(CompanyAddr,RespCenter);
                             CompanyInfo."Phone No." := RespCenter."Phone No.";
                             CompanyInfo."Fax No." := RespCenter."Fax No.";
                           END ELSE BEGIN
                             FormatAddr.Company(CompanyAddr,CompanyInfo);
                           END;

                           PostedDocDim1.SETRANGE("Table ID",DATABASE::"Purch. Cr. Memo Hdr.");
                           PostedDocDim1.SETRANGE("Document No.","Purch. Cr. Memo Hdr."."No.");

                           IF "Return Order No." = '' THEN
                             ReturnOrderNoText := ''
                           ELSE
                             ReturnOrderNoText := FIELDCAPTION("Return Order No.");
                           IF "Purchaser Code" = '' THEN BEGIN
                             SalesPurchPerson.INIT;
                             PurchaserText := '';
                           END ELSE BEGIN
                             SalesPurchPerson.GET("Purchaser Code");
                             PurchaserText := Text000
                           END;
                           IF "Your Reference" = '' THEN
                             ReferenceText := ''
                           ELSE
                             ReferenceText := FIELDCAPTION("Your Reference");
                           IF "VAT Registration No." = '' THEN
                             VATNoText := ''
                           ELSE
                             VATNoText := FIELDCAPTION("VAT Registration No.");
                           IF "Currency Code" = '' THEN BEGIN
                             GLSetup.TESTFIELD("LCY Code");
                             TotalText := STRSUBSTNO(Text001,GLSetup."LCY Code");
                             TotalInclVATText := STRSUBSTNO(Text002,GLSetup."LCY Code");
                             TotalExclVATText := STRSUBSTNO(Text007,GLSetup."LCY Code");
                           END ELSE BEGIN
                             TotalText := STRSUBSTNO(Text001,"Currency Code");
                             TotalInclVATText := STRSUBSTNO(Text002,"Currency Code");
                             TotalExclVATText := STRSUBSTNO(Text007,"Currency Code");
                           END;
                           FormatAddr.PurchCrMemoPayTo(VendAddr,"Purch. Cr. Memo Hdr.");
                           IF "Applies-to Doc. No." = '' THEN
                             AppliedToText := ''
                           ELSE
                             AppliedToText := FORMAT(STRSUBSTNO(Text003,FORMAT("Applies-to Doc. Type"),"Applies-to Doc. No."));

                           FormatAddr.PurchCrMemoShipTo(ShipToAddr,"Purch. Cr. Memo Hdr.");

                           IF LogInteraction THEN
                             IF NOT CurrReport.PREVIEW THEN
                               SegManagement.LogDocument(
                                 16,"No.",0,0,DATABASE::Vendor,"Buy-from Vendor No.","Purchaser Code",'',"Posting Description",'');

                           IF ISSERVICETIER THEN
                             PricesIncludingVAT := FORMAT("Purch. Cr. Memo Hdr."."Prices Including VAT");

                           CLEAR(G_TitreAnaLyse);
                           G_recLivBord.RESET;
                           G_recLivBord.SETRANGE("Document No.","Purch. Cr. Memo Hdr."."No.");
                           G_recLivBord.SETRANGE(Type,G_recLivBord.Type::Item);
                           G_recLivBord.SETFILTER("No.",'<>%1',' ');
                           G_recLivBord.SETFILTER("Collection recept No." ,'<>%1',' ');
                           IF G_recLivBord.FINDFIRST THEN
                           BEGIN
                               G_recPostAnalyse.RESET;
                               G_recPostAnalyse.SETRANGE("Receipt Type",G_recPostAnalyse."Receipt Type"::Receipt);
                               G_recPostAnalyse.SETRANGE("Receipt No.",G_recLivBord."Collection recept No.");
                               IF G_recPostAnalyse.FINDSET THEN BEGIN
                                 G_NumAnalyse:=G_recPostAnalyse.COUNT;
                                 L_i:=1;
                                 REPEAT
                                   G_TitreAnaLyse[L_i]:=FORMAT(G_recPostAnalyse."Analysis Code");
                                   L_i:=L_i+1;
                                 UNTIL G_recPostAnalyse.NEXT=0;
                               END;

                           END;

                           G_MttLine:=0;
                           G_MttIncVat:=0;
                           G_Qt'Normes:=0;
                           G_Qt'Livr'es:=0;

                           CLEAR(G_TaxesText);
                           CLEAR(G_TaxesMttUnit);
                           CLEAR(G_TaxesMtt);
                           CLEAR (G_ResulAnalyse);

                           G_MttTotalTaxe:=0;

                           G_MttComp:=0;
                           G_MttFourLett:=0;
                           G_MttDEtte:=0;

                           G_TextPaymentMethod:='';
                           IF G_recPaymentMeth.GET("Purch. Cr. Memo Hdr."."Payment Method Code")
                           THEN G_TextPaymentMethod:=STRSUBSTNO(Text8053000,G_recPaymentMeth.Description);

                           G_TextBordereau:=Text8053001;
                           IF "Purch. Cr. Memo Hdr."."Collection Invoice Type"="Purch. Cr. Memo Hdr."."Collection Invoice Type"::Supplement
                           THEN
                           G_TextBordereau:=STRSUBSTNO('%1 %2',G_TextBordereau,Text8053002);

                           TotalAmount := 0;
                           TotalAmountIncVAT := 0;
                           TotalLineAmount := 0;
                           TotalInvDiscountAmount := 0;
                         END;

        ReqFilterFields=No.,Buy-from Vendor No.,No. Printed;
      }

    { PROPERTIES
      {
        DataItemIndent=3;
        DataItemTable=Table2000000026;
        DataItemTableView=SORTING(Num'ro)
                          WHERE(Num'ro=FILTER(1..));
        DataItemVarName=DimensionLoop1;
        OnPreDataItem=BEGIN
                        IF NOT ShowInternalInfo THEN
                          CurrReport.BREAK;
                      END;

        OnAfterGetRecord=BEGIN
                           IF Num'ro = 1 THEN BEGIN
                             IF NOT PostedDocDim1.FIND('-') THEN
                               CurrReport.BREAK;
                           END ELSE
                             IF NOT Continue THEN
                               CurrReport.BREAK;

                           CLEAR(DimText);
                           Continue := FALSE;
                           REPEAT
                             OldDimText := DimText;
                             IF DimText = '' THEN
                               DimText := STRSUBSTNO(
                                 '%1 %2',PostedDocDim1."Dimension Code",PostedDocDim1."Dimension Value Code")
                             ELSE
                               DimText :=
                                 STRSUBSTNO(
                                   '%1, %2 %3',DimText,
                                   PostedDocDim1."Dimension Code",PostedDocDim1."Dimension Value Code");
                             IF STRLEN(DimText) > MAXSTRLEN(OldDimText) THEN BEGIN
                               DimText := OldDimText;
                               Continue := TRUE;
                               EXIT;
                             END;
                           UNTIL (PostedDocDim1.NEXT = 0);
                         END;

        DataItemLinkReference=Purch. Cr. Memo Hdr.;
      }

    { PROPERTIES
      {
        DataItemIndent=3;
        DataItemTable=Table125;
        DataItemTableView=SORTING(Document No.,Line No.);
        OnPreDataItem=VAR
                        PurchCrMemoLine@1000 : Record 125;
                        VATIdentifier@1001 : Code[10];
                      BEGIN
                        VATAmountLine.RESET;
                        VATAmountLine.DELETEALL;

                        VATAmountLinebIS.RESET;
                        VATAmountLinebIS.DELETEALL;

                        MoreLines := FIND('+');
                        WHILE MoreLines AND (Description = '') AND ("No." = '') AND (Quantity = 0) AND (Amount = 0) DO
                          MoreLines := NEXT(-1) <> 0;
                        IF NOT MoreLines THEN
                          CurrReport.BREAK;
                        SETRANGE("Line No.",0,"Line No.");
                        CurrReport.CREATETOTALS("Line Amount",Amount,"Amount Including VAT","Inv. Discount Amount");

                        IF ISSERVICETIER THEN BEGIN
                          PurchCrMemoLine.SETRANGE("Document No.","Purch. Cr. Memo Hdr."."No.");
                          PurchCrMemoLine.SETFILTER(Type,'<>%1',0);
                          VATAmountText :='';
                          IF PurchCrMemoLine.FIND('-') THEN BEGIN
                            VATAmountText := STRSUBSTNO(Text012,PurchCrMemoLine."VAT %");
                            VATIdentifier := PurchCrMemoLine."VAT Identifier";
                            REPEAT
                              IF (PurchCrMemoLine."VAT Identifier" <> VATIdentifier) AND (PurchCrMemoLine.Quantity <> 0) THEN
                                VATAmountText := Text013;
                            UNTIL PurchCrMemoLine.NEXT = 0;
                          END;
                          AllowInvDiscount := FORMAT("Purch. Cr. Memo Line"."Allow Invoice Disc.");
                        END;

                        CurrReport.CREATETOTALS(G_MttLine,G_MttIncVat,G_Qt'Livr'es,Quantity);
                      END;

        OnAfterGetRecord=VAR
                           l_j@8053000 : Integer;
                           L_i@8053001 : Integer;
                           L_recPurchLineVariatPrice@8053002 : Record 8053005;
                           AnalyseCharacteristic_L@8053003 : Record 8053092;
                         BEGIN
                           IF (Type = Type::"G/L Account") AND (NOT ShowInternalInfo) THEN
                             "No." := '';

                           IF "Line Collection Type"="Line Collection Type"::Tax
                           THEN BEGIN
                             VATAmountLinebIS.INIT;
                             VATAmountLinebIS."VAT Identifier" := "VAT Identifier";
                             VATAmountLinebIS."VAT Calculation Type" := "VAT Calculation Type";
                             VATAmountLinebIS."Tax Group Code" := "Tax Group Code";
                             VATAmountLinebIS."Use Tax" := "Use Tax";
                             VATAmountLinebIS."VAT %" := "VAT %";
                             VATAmountLinebIS."VAT Base" := Amount;
                             VATAmountLinebIS."Amount Including VAT" := "Amount Including VAT";
                             VATAmountLinebIS."Line Amount" := "Line Amount";
                             IF "Allow Invoice Disc." THEN
                               VATAmountLinebIS."Inv. Disc. Base Amount" := "Line Amount";
                             VATAmountLinebIS."Invoice Discount Amount" := "Inv. Discount Amount";
                             VATAmountLinebIS.InsertLine;

                             l_j:=0;
                             REPEAT
                              l_j:=l_j+1;
                              IF G_TaxesText[l_j]=''
                                THEN BEGIN
                                  G_TaxesText[l_j]:=Description;
                                  //G_TaxesMttUnit[l_j]:="Purch. Inv. Line"."Direct Unit Cost";
                                  //G_TaxesMtt[l_j]:="Purch. Inv. Line".Amount;
                                   G_TaxesMttUnit[l_j]:="Amount Including VAT"/Quantity;
                                   G_TaxesMtt[l_j]:="Amount Including VAT"
                                END ELSE BEGIN
                                  IF G_TaxesText[l_j]=Description
                                  THEN BEGIN
                                    G_TaxesMtt[l_j]:=G_TaxesMtt[l_j]+"Amount Including VAT"
                                  END;

                                END;
                             UNTIL (G_TaxesText[l_j]=Description) OR (l_j=11);


                           END ELSE BEGIN

                             VATAmountLine.INIT;
                             VATAmountLine."VAT Identifier" := "Purch. Cr. Memo Line"."VAT Identifier";
                             VATAmountLine."VAT Calculation Type" := "VAT Calculation Type";
                             VATAmountLine."Tax Group Code" := "Tax Group Code";
                             VATAmountLine."Use Tax" := "Use Tax";
                             VATAmountLine."VAT %" := "VAT %";
                             VATAmountLine."VAT Base" := Amount;
                             VATAmountLine."Amount Including VAT" := "Amount Including VAT";
                             VATAmountLine."Line Amount" := "Line Amount";
                             IF "Allow Invoice Disc." THEN
                               VATAmountLine."Inv. Disc. Base Amount" := "Line Amount";
                             VATAmountLine."Invoice Discount Amount" := "Inv. Discount Amount";
                             VATAmountLine.InsertLine;

                             AllowInvDiscount := FORMAT("Purch. Cr. Memo Line"."Allow Invoice Disc.");

                             G_MttIncVat:=G_MttIncVat+"Amount Including VAT";

                           END;

                           IF ("Collection recept No."<>'') AND (Type=Type::Item)
                           THEN BEGIN
                             G_recPostReceip.INIT;
                             IF G_recPostReceip.GET(G_recPostReceip."Receipt Type"::Receipt,"Collection recept No.") THEN;
                             //

                             G_Qt'Livr'es:=G_Qt'Livr'es+G_recPostReceip."Net Weight";

                             //R'sultats d'analyseclear
                             CLEAR(G_ResulAnalyse);
                             G_recPostAnalyse.RESET;
                             G_recPostAnalyse.SETRANGE("Receipt Type",G_recPostAnalyse."Receipt Type"::Receipt);
                             G_recPostAnalyse.SETRANGE("Receipt No.","Collection recept No.");
                             IF G_recPostAnalyse.FINDSET THEN BEGIN
                               L_i:=1;
                               REPEAT
                                 G_ResulAnalyse[L_i]:=G_recPostAnalyse.Value;
                                 L_i:=L_i+1;
                               UNTIL G_recPostAnalyse.NEXT=0;
                             END;

                           //PRESTATIONS\\

                           G_MttTransport:=0;
                           G_MttTransportUNit:=0;
                           G_MttDisinsect:=0;
                           G_MttDisinsectUnit:=0;
                           G_MttSechage:=0;
                           G_MttSechageUnit:=0;
                           G_MttStockFixe:=0;
                           G_MttStockFixeUnit:=0;
                           G_MttStockVariable:=0;
                           G_MttStockVariableUnit:=0;
                           G_MttAdmin:=0;
                           G_MttAdminUnit:=0;


                           //transport
                             G_recLivBord.RESET;
                             G_recLivBord.SETRANGE("Document No.","Document No.");
                             G_recLivBord.SETRANGE("Collection recept No.","Collection recept No.");
                             G_recLivBord.SETRANGE("Line Collection Type",G_recLivBord."Line Collection Type"::Shipping);
                             IF G_recLivBord.FINDFIRST
                             THEN BEGIN
                               G_MttTransport:=G_recLivBord.Amount;
                               G_MttTransportUNit:=G_recLivBord."Direct Unit Cost";
                             END;

                           //D'sinsectisation
                             G_recLivBord.RESET;
                             G_recLivBord.SETRANGE("Document No.","Document No.");
                             G_recLivBord.SETRANGE("Collection recept No.","Collection recept No.");
                             G_recLivBord.SETRANGE("Line Collection Type",G_recLivBord."Line Collection Type"::Disinsection);
                             IF G_recLivBord.FINDFIRST
                             THEN BEGIN
                               G_MttDisinsect:=G_recLivBord.Amount;
                               G_MttDisinsectUnit:=G_recLivBord."Direct Unit Cost";
                             END;


                           //Stock fixe
                             G_recLivBord.RESET;
                             G_recLivBord.SETRANGE("Document No.","Document No.");
                             G_recLivBord.SETRANGE("Collection recept No.","Collection recept No.");
                             G_recLivBord.SETRANGE("Line Collection Type",G_recLivBord."Line Collection Type"::"Fixe Storage");
                             IF G_recLivBord.FINDFIRST
                             THEN BEGIN
                               G_MttStockFixe:=G_recLivBord.Amount;
                               G_MttStockFixeUnit:=G_recLivBord."Direct Unit Cost";
                             END;

                           //stock variable
                             G_recLivBord.RESET;
                             G_recLivBord.SETRANGE("Document No.","Document No.");
                             G_recLivBord.SETRANGE("Collection recept No.","Collection recept No.");
                             G_recLivBord.SETRANGE("Line Collection Type",G_recLivBord."Line Collection Type"::"Variable Storage");
                             IF G_recLivBord.FINDFIRST
                             THEN BEGIN
                               G_MttStockVariable:=G_recLivBord.Amount;
                               G_MttStockVariableUnit:=G_recLivBord."Direct Unit Cost";
                             END;

                           //Administratif
                             G_recLivBord.RESET;
                             G_recLivBord.SETRANGE("Document No.","Document No.");
                             G_recLivBord.SETRANGE("Collection recept No.","Collection recept No.");
                             G_recLivBord.SETRANGE("Line Collection Type",G_recLivBord."Line Collection Type"::Administrative);
                             IF G_recLivBord.FINDFIRST
                             THEN BEGIN
                               G_MttAdmin:=G_recLivBord.Amount;
                               G_MttAdminUnit:=G_recLivBord."Direct Unit Cost";
                             END;

                           //S'chage
                             G_recLivBord.RESET;
                             G_recLivBord.SETRANGE("Document No.","Document No.");
                             G_recLivBord.SETRANGE("Collection recept No.","Collection recept No.");
                             G_recLivBord.SETRANGE("Line Collection Type",G_recLivBord."Line Collection Type"::Drying);
                             IF G_recLivBord.FINDFIRST
                             THEN BEGIN
                               G_MttSechage:=G_recLivBord.Amount;
                               G_MttSechageUnit:=G_recLivBord."Direct Unit Cost";
                             END;

                           //RECHERCHE DES REFACTIONS\\
                            G_MttRefacPSUnit:=0;
                            G_MttRefacHumiUnit:=0;
                            G_MttRefacImpurUnit:=0;
                            G_MttRefacProteUnit:=0;

                           //PS
                             L_recPurchLineVariatPrice.RESET;
                             L_recPurchLineVariatPrice.SETRANGE("Document Type",L_recPurchLineVariatPrice."Document Type"::"Posted invoice");
                             L_recPurchLineVariatPrice.SETRANGE("Document No.","Document No.");
                             L_recPurchLineVariatPrice.SETRANGE("Purch Line No.","Line No.");
                           //
                             IF  L_recPurchLineVariatPrice.FINDSET THEN
                               REPEAT
                                 IF AnalyseCharacteristic_L.GET(L_recPurchLineVariatPrice."Analysis Code")
                                 AND
                                   ((AnalyseCharacteristic_L."Analyse Code Indentifiant"='PS') OR (AnalyseCharacteristic_L."Analyse Code Indentifiant"='H')
                                     OR  (AnalyseCharacteristic_L."Analyse Code Indentifiant"='I') OR
                                       (AnalyseCharacteristic_L."Analyse Code Indentifiant"='PR'))
                                 THEN BEGIN
                                     CASE AnalyseCharacteristic_L."Analyse Code Indentifiant" OF
                                      'PS': G_MttRefacPSUnit:=L_recPurchLineVariatPrice."Unit Price Variation";
                                       'H': G_MttRefacHumiUnit:=L_recPurchLineVariatPrice."Unit Price Variation";
                                       'I': G_MttRefacImpurUnit:=L_recPurchLineVariatPrice."Unit Price Variation";
                                      'PR': G_MttRefacProteUnit:=L_recPurchLineVariatPrice."Unit Price Variation";
                                      END;
                                 END ELSE
                                   G_MttAutreUnit:=G_MttAutreUnit+L_recPurchLineVariatPrice."Unit Price Variation";
                               UNTIL L_recPurchLineVariatPrice.NEXT=0;

                           //


                           {
                           //Humidit'
                             L_recPurchLineVariatPrice.SETRANGE("Analysis Code",L_recPurchLineVariatPrice."Analysis Code"::"1");
                             IF L_recPurchLineVariatPrice.FINDFIRST
                               THEN G_MttRefacHumiUnit:=L_recPurchLineVariatPrice."Unit Price Variation";

                           //Impuret'
                             L_recPurchLineVariatPrice.SETRANGE("Analysis Code",L_recPurchLineVariatPrice."Analysis Code"::"2");
                             IF L_recPurchLineVariatPrice.FINDFIRST
                               THEN G_MttRefacImpurUnit:=L_recPurchLineVariatPrice."Unit Price Variation";

                           //Prot'ine
                             L_recPurchLineVariatPrice.SETRANGE("Analysis Code",L_recPurchLineVariatPrice."Analysis Code"::"3");
                             IF L_recPurchLineVariatPrice.FINDFIRST
                               THEN G_MttRefacProteUnit:=L_recPurchLineVariatPrice."Unit Price Variation";
                            }


                           //Edition du "Autres"
                             G_MttAutreUnit:=0;
                             G_MttAutreUnit:=G_MttDisinsectUnit+G_MttAdminUnit+G_MttRefacHumiUnit+G_MttRefacImpurUnit;


                           //Montant Ligne
                             G_MttLine:="Line Amount"+G_MttTransport+G_MttDisinsect+G_MttSechage+G_MttStockFixe+G_MttStockVariable+G_MttAdmin;

                             G_PULine:=0;
                             IF Quantity<>0 THEN BEGIN
                               G_PULine:=(G_MttLine/Quantity);
                               G_PULine:=ROUND(G_PULine,0.01);
                             END;
                           G_Qt'Normes:=G_Qt'Normes+Quantity;
                           END;

                           LineType := Type;

                           TotalDeliveredQty += G_Qt'Livr'es;
                           TotalMttLine += G_MttLine;
                           TotalMttIncVAT += G_MttIncVat;

                           TotalAmount += Amount;
                           TotalAmountIncVAT += "Amount Including VAT";
                           TotalLineAmount += "Line Amount";
                           TotalInvDiscountAmount += "Inv. Discount Amount";

                           IF ISSERVICETIER AND (G_TextTVA <> 'Multi taux') THEN BEGIN
                              IF (VATAmountLine."VAT Identifier" <> PreviousVatIdentifier) AND (PreviousVatIdentifier <> '') THEN
                                 G_TextTVA := 'Multi taux'
                              ELSE
                                 G_TextTVA := STRSUBSTNO('%1%',FORMAT(VATAmountLine."VAT %"));
                              PreviousVatIdentifier := VATAmountLine."VAT Identifier";
                           END;
                         END;

        TotalFields=Line Amount;
        DataItemLinkReference=Purch. Cr. Memo Hdr.;
        DataItemLink=Document No.=FIELD(No.);
      }
      SECTIONS

    { PROPERTIES
      {
        DataItemIndent=4;
        DataItemTable=Table2000000026;
        DataItemTableView=SORTING(Num'ro)
                          WHERE(Num'ro=FILTER(1..));
        DataItemVarName=DimensionLoop2;
        OnPreDataItem=BEGIN
                        IF NOT ShowInternalInfo THEN
                          CurrReport.BREAK;

                        PostedDocDim2.SETRANGE("Table ID",DATABASE::"Purch. Cr. Memo Line");
                        PostedDocDim2.SETRANGE("Document No.","Purch. Cr. Memo Line"."Document No.");
                        PostedDocDim2.SETRANGE("Line No.","Purch. Cr. Memo Line"."Line No.");
                      END;

        OnAfterGetRecord=BEGIN
                           IF Num'ro = 1 THEN BEGIN
                             IF NOT PostedDocDim2.FIND('-') THEN
                               CurrReport.BREAK;
                           END ELSE
                             IF NOT Continue THEN
                               CurrReport.BREAK;

                           CLEAR(DimText);
                           Continue := FALSE;
                           REPEAT
                             OldDimText := DimText;
                             IF DimText = '' THEN
                               DimText := STRSUBSTNO(
                                 '%1 %2',PostedDocDim2."Dimension Code",PostedDocDim2."Dimension Value Code")
                             ELSE
                               DimText :=
                                 STRSUBSTNO(
                                   '%1, %2 %3',DimText,
                                   PostedDocDim2."Dimension Code",PostedDocDim2."Dimension Value Code");
                             IF STRLEN(DimText) > MAXSTRLEN(OldDimText) THEN BEGIN
                               DimText := OldDimText;
                               Continue := TRUE;
                               EXIT;
                             END;
                           UNTIL (PostedDocDim2.NEXT = 0);
                         END;

      }
      SECTIONS
      {

            SectionType=Body;
            SectionWidth=28442;

    { PROPERTIES
      {
        DataItemIndent=3;
        DataItemTable=Table2000000026;
        DataItemTableView=SORTING(Num'ro);
        DataItemVarName=VATCounterLCY;
        OnPreDataItem=BEGIN
                        IF (NOT GLSetup."Print VAT specification in LCY") OR
                           ("Purch. Cr. Memo Hdr."."Currency Code"  = '') OR
                           (VATAmountLine.GetTotalVATAmount = 0) THEN
                          CurrReport.BREAK;

                        SETRANGE(Num'ro,1,VATAmountLine.COUNT);
                        CurrReport.CREATETOTALS(VALVATBaseLCY,VALVATAmountLCY);

                        IF GLSetup."LCY Code" = '' THEN
                          VALSpecLCYHeader := Text008 + Text009
                        ELSE
                          VALSpecLCYHeader := Text008 + FORMAT(GLSetup."LCY Code");

                        CurrExchRate.FindCurrency("Purch. Cr. Memo Hdr."."Posting Date","Purch. Cr. Memo Hdr."."Currency Code",1);
                        CalculatedExchRate := ROUND(1 / "Purch. Cr. Memo Hdr."."Currency Factor" * CurrExchRate."Exchange Rate Amount",0.000001);
                        VALExchRate := STRSUBSTNO(Text010,CalculatedExchRate,CurrExchRate."Exchange Rate Amount");
                      END;

        OnAfterGetRecord=BEGIN
                           VATAmountLine.GetLine(Num'ro);

                           VALVATBaseLCY := ROUND(VATAmountLine."VAT Base" / "Purch. Cr. Memo Hdr."."Currency Factor");
                           VALVATAmountLCY := ROUND(VATAmountLine."VAT Amount" / "Purch. Cr. Memo Hdr."."Currency Factor");
                         END;

      }

    { PROPERTIES
      {
        DataItemIndent=3;
        DataItemTable=Table2000000026;
        DataItemTableView=SORTING(Num'ro)
                          WHERE(Num'ro=CONST(1));
        DataItemVarName=Total;
        OnPreDataItem=BEGIN
                        //IF "Purch. Cr. Memo Hdr."."Buy-from Vendor No." = "Purch. Cr. Memo Hdr."."Pay-to Vendor No." THEN
                        //  CurrReport.BREAK;

                        G_MttTvaTaxe :=0;
                        IF VATAmountLinebIS.FIND('-')
                        THEN
                          REPEAT
                            G_MttTvaTaxe :=G_MttTvaTaxe + VATAmountLinebIS."VAT Amount";
                          UNTIL VATAmountLinebIS.NEXT=0;

                        G_MttTotalTaxe:=//G_MttTvaTaxe+
                        G_TaxesMtt[1]+G_TaxesMtt[2]+G_TaxesMtt[3]+G_TaxesMtt[4]+G_TaxesMtt[5]+
                             G_TaxesMtt[6]+G_TaxesMtt[7]+G_TaxesMtt[8]+G_TaxesMtt[9]+G_TaxesMtt[10];


                        G_recCompEntr.RESET;
                        G_recCompEntr.SETRANGE("SettOff Type",G_recCompEntr."SettOff Type"::Posted);
                        G_recCompEntr.SETRANGE("Invoice No","Purch. Cr. Memo Hdr."."No.");
                        G_recCompEntr.SETRANGE("Entry Type",G_recCompEntr."Entry Type"::Customer);
                        G_recCompEntr.CALCSUMS(Amount);
                        G_MttComp:=G_recCompEntr.Amount;

                        G_recCompEntr.RESET;
                        G_recCompEntr.SETRANGE("SettOff Type",G_recCompEntr."SettOff Type"::Posted);
                        G_recCompEntr.SETRANGE("Invoice No","Purch. Cr. Memo Hdr."."No.");
                        G_recCompEntr.SETRANGE("Entry Type",G_recCompEntr."Entry Type"::Vendor);
                        G_recCompEntr.CALCSUMS(Amount);
                        G_MttFourLett:=G_recCompEntr.Amount;

                        G_recCompEntr.RESET;
                        G_recCompEntr.SETRANGE("SettOff Type",G_recCompEntr."SettOff Type"::Posted);
                        G_recCompEntr.SETRANGE("Invoice No","Purch. Cr. Memo Hdr."."No.");
                        G_recCompEntr.SETRANGE("Entry Type",G_recCompEntr."Entry Type"::Loan);
                        G_recCompEntr.CALCSUMS(Amount);
                        G_MttDEtte:=G_recCompEntr.Amount;
                      END;

      }

  <Code>Public Function BlankZero(ByVal Value As Decimal)
    if Value = 0 then
        Return ""
    end if
    Return Value
End Function

Public Function BlankPos(ByVal Value As Decimal)
    if Value &gt; 0 then
        Return ""
    end if
    Return Value
End Function

Public Function BlankZeroAndPos(ByVal Value As Decimal)
    if Value &gt;= 0 then
        Return ""
    end if
    Return Value
End Function

Public Function BlankNeg(ByVal Value As Decimal)
    if Value &lt; 0 then
        Return ""
    end if
    Return Value
End Function

Public Function BlankNegAndZero(ByVal Value As Decimal)
    if Value &lt;= 0 then
        Return ""
    end if
    Return Value
End Function




Shared Data1 as Object
Shared Data2 as Object
Shared Data3 as Object
Shared Data4 as Object

                Public Function SetData(NewData as Object,Group as integer)
                If Group = 1 and NewData &gt; "" Then
                Data1 = NewData
                End If
                If Group = 2 and NewData &gt; "" Then
                Data2 = NewData
                End If
                If Group = 3 and NewData &gt; "" Then
                Data3 = NewData
                End If
                If Group = 4 and NewData &gt; "" Then
                Data4 = NewData
                End If
                End Function

                Public Function GetData(Num as Integer, Group as integer) as Object
                if Group = 1 then
                Return Cstr(Choose(Num, Split(Cstr(Data1),Chr(177))))
                End If
                if Group = 2 then
                Return Cstr(Choose(Num, Split(Cstr(Data2),Chr(177))))
                End If
                if Group = 3 then
                Return Cstr(Choose(Num, Split(Cstr(Data3),Chr(177))))
                End If
                if Group = 4 then
                Return Cstr(Choose(Num, Split(Cstr(Data4),Chr(177))))
                End If
                End Function

Shared offset as Integer
Shared newPage as Object
Shared currentgroup1 as Object
Shared currentgroup2 as Object
Shared currentgroup3 as Object

Public Function GetGroupPageNumber(NewPage as Boolean, pagenumber as Integer) as Object
  If NewPage
    offset = pagenumber - 1
  End If
  Return pagenumber - offset
End Function

Public Function IsNewPage(group1 as Object, group2 as Object, group3 as Object) As Boolean
newPage = FALSE
If Not (group1 = currentgroup1)
    newPage = TRUE
    currentgroup1 = group1
    currentgroup2 = group2
    currentgroup3 = group3
     ELSE 
      If Not (group2 = currentgroup2)
        newPage = TRUE 
        currentgroup2 = group2
        currentgroup3 = group3
        ELSE
          If Not (group3 = currentgroup3)
          newPage = TRUE 
          currentgroup3 = group3
         End If
     End If
  End If
Return newPage
End Function


Public Function SetLanguage( Language As String)     
   System.Threading.Thread.CurrentThread.CurrentCulture = New System.Globalization.CultureInfo(Language)
End Function</Code>
